Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the asteroids object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 456. The moduli space has dimension at most 545 with 4 connected components.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 456 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the asteroids structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 545. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 4. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.

Theorem 214.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: The asteroids operator L associated with structural decomposition theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 502. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 216 with 5 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the structural decomposition condition on asteroids structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 502. The convergence follows from the structural decomposition estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 216. QED.
